tūvaṟ kalitta putumukai yūṉcettu"	(malaipaṭu. 145, 146)
eṉavum varum. piṟavumaṉṉa.	(16)
iṉṉa uvamattukku iṉṉa vāypāṭu uriya eṉpatu
varalāṟṟu muṟaiyāṉ aṟiyappaṭumāṟu
292.	tatta marapiṉ tōṉṟumaṉ poruḷē.
itu, nāṉku uvamattukkum orōvoṉṟu eṭṭākak kūṟiya vāy pāṭṭiṟkellām puṟaṉaṭai.
(i-ḷ.) iṉṉataṟku iṉṉa vāypāṭu uriyaveṉṟaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉaiyeṉṟārkku, avai tattam varalāṟṟu muṟaimaiyāṉē avvap poruṭōṉṟa niṟkum e-ṟu.
"pulipāyntāṅkup pāyntā'ṉeṉa viṉaiyuvamattiṟku vanta āṅkeṉ kiḷavi 'taḷirāṅkuc civantamēṉi' yeṉiṟ poruntātu.
iṉi, eḷḷaveṉpatu payaṉuvamattiṟku ēṟkumeṉṟāṉ; atu 'puli yeḷḷum pāyttu'ḷeṉalākātu; eṉṉai? pulikku vali kūṟiṉaṉṟi ataṉōṭu uvamikkappaṭṭa cāttaṟkup pukaḻākātākalā ṉeṉpatu. oruvaṉai veṉṟi kūṟuṅkāl avaṉōṭu oppikkiṉṟa puliyēṟṟiṉai avaṉaik kaṇṭu etirniṟkalāṟṟātu puṟaṅkoṭuttateṉalum, naṭuṅkiṟ ṟeṉaluṅ, kaṇ cimpuḷittateṉalum, avaṟkup pukaḻāmeṉpatē karutik kūṟiṉ, kūṟiṉa uvama veḷippāṭumiṉṟi avaṟkatu pukaḻumākāteṉpatu karuttu.
iṉi, maḻaiyiṉaik koṭaikku iḻittuc collavum peṟupa vaṉṟē? avvāṟu colliṉum ataṉai avaṉiṉ iḻittu nōkkātu ulakamātaliṉ.
viḻaiya vīḻa pulla matippa vella eṉpaṉavum uvamaitāṉ poruḷai yottaṟ kavāviṉaveṉṟu poruḷ tōṉṟa niṟkumeṉṟa vāṟu. kaḷḷaveṉpatūum ataṉ kuṇam ataṉkaṇ illāmaṟ katuviṟṟeṉṉum poruṭṭu. poruvaveṉpatūum maḻaiyiṉaiyum oruvaṉaiyum uṟaḻun tuṇaic ciṟantāṉeṉac collutal. ivvāṟṟāṉ ivai urimai kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉi, meyyuvamattiṟku uriyaveṉappaṭṭaṉavum avvāṟē oru kāraṇamuṭaiya pōlum. kaṭuppa eṉṟakkāl viṉaikkum payattiṟkum ēlātu; eṉṉai? kaṭuttaleṉpatu aiyuṟutal. puliyoṭu maṟavaṉai aiyuṟavēṇṭuva toru kāraṇamiṉmaiyāṉum, maḻaiyiṉ viḷaitta payattōṭu uvamittaliṉ maḻaiyōṭu oruvaṉai aiyañceytal vēṇṭuvatiṉmaiyāṉum, vaṭivukaṇṭavaḻi aiyam piṟakkumākalāṉum, uruveṉpatu kuṇamākalāṟ poruḷvēṟṟumai aṟintavaḻi aiyañ cellātākalāṉum, meykkurimai kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. ēyttaleṉpa tūum poruntutalākaliṉ vaṭiviṟkēṟkum. maruḷa puraiya oṭṭa oṭuṅka eṉpaṉavuṅ kaṭuttalpōlum poruṇmaiya; eṉṉai? maruṭciyum puraiyuṇarvuṅ kavarttalaik kāṭṭutalāṉum, oṭṭa oṭuṅka eṉpaṉavum iraṇṭaṉaiyum oṉṟeṉṉum poruṇmaiyavākalāṉu meṉpatu, nikarttalum avviṉap poruḷeṉpataṉaik kāṭṭutaliṉ vaṭiviṟ kēṟṟatu. ōṭaveṉpatum ōṭutaṟṟoḻil vaṭiviṟ kallatiṉmaiyiṉ av vaṭiviṟkēṟṟatu. paṇpāyiṟ paṇpu niṟappaṇpu, ōṭiṟṟeṉalākāmaiyiṉ.
iṉi, uruvuvama vāypāṭṭiṟkuṅ kāraṇaṅ kūṟuṅkāṟ pōlu meṉpatu iṭaiccollākalāṉum, marīivanta viṉaippāṟ paṭṭatāka lāṉum, ataṟkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟappaṭāteṉpatu. aḵtēl, ataṉai ivveṭṭaṟkum muṉpu kūṟiyateṉṉai, poruḷuṭaiyavaṟṟaip piṟkūṟi yeṉiṉ, atuvum aṉṉa veṉpatupōla maṟṟai mūṉṟu uvamattum payiṉṟuvarumeṉpatu eytuvit taṟkeṉpatu. maṟuppa oppa eṉpaṉa mutalāyiṉavum oru kāraṇamuṭaiya veṉpatu āciriyaṉ peruvaraviṉa vāka urimaippaṭuttuk kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ aṟintām. allatūum 'marapiṟ ṟōṉṟum' eṉṟataṉāṉ ivaiyellām marapupaṟṟi aṟiyal vēṇṭum eṉavē, talaiccaṅkattār mutalāyiṉār ceyyuḷkaḷuḷ avvāṟu payiṉṟu varumeṉpatu aṟintāmaṉṟē, ivvāṟu cūttirañ ceytalā ṉeṉpatu.	(17)
uvamam eṭṭākum eṉal
293.	nāliraṇ ṭākum pālumā ruṇṭē
ituvum mēlaṉavaṟṟaiyē pakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉai payaṉ mey uru eṉappaṭṭa nāṉkum eṭṭām pakutiyum uṇṭu e-ṟu.
avai uvamattokai nāṉkum uvamaviri nāṉkumeṉa eṭṭātalum uṭaiyaveṉṟavāṟu. avai puliyaṉṉa pāyttuḷ 'pulippāyttuḷ' eṉavum, maḻaiyaṉṉa vaṇkai 'maḻai vaṇkai' eṉavum, vēyaṉṉatōḷ 'vēyt tōḷ' eṉavum, pavaḷattaṉṉavāy 'pavaḷavāy' eṉavum tokaiviripaṟṟi nāṉkum eṭṭāyiṉavāṟu. collōttiṉuḷ ivvāypāṭu virintu varumāṟu kūṟātu āṇṭut tokaiyārāyccippaṭṭa māttiraiyāṉē kūṟiṉāṉ. avvuvamantāṉ īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭutal poruḷiṉatākalāṉum, iṭaiccollēyaṉṟip poruḷ payappaṉavum avvurupākalāṉum, ataṉ viriviṉai īṇṭup peyartantu kūṟi ataṉ tokaiyoṭu paṭuppa ittuṇaip pakutiyavām annālvakai yuvamamum eṉṟāṉeṉpatu.
ituvumoru karuttu: muṉṉar evveṭṭākak kūṟiyavai ovvoṉṟum ivviraṇṭu kūṟāki eṭṭām pakutiyuṭaiya eṉṟavāṟu. yāṅkaṉameṉiṉ, aṉṉa eṉṉuñ colmutalākiya eṭṭaṉuḷ, aṉṉa āṅka māṉa eṉṉa eṉappaṭṭa nāṉkum vēṟoru poruḷai uṇarttā maiyiṉ ōriṉamāki oṉṟākavum, viṟappa uṟaḻa takaiya nōkka eṉṉum nāṉkum oru poruḷuṭaimaiyiṉ ōriṉamākavum, ivvāṟē iṉam nōkkutaṟ kuṟippiṉa vāyiṉa. ivvāṟṟāṉ iraṇṭeṉavum paṭum eṭṭumeṉṟavāṟu. itaṉatu payaṉ ivviraṇṭu kūṟṟāṉ aṭakkappaṭum viṉaiyuvamaccol eṭṭum (287) eṉṟavāṟu. viṟattal, iṉamākac ceṟiyumeṉṉum poruṭṭu. uṟaḻcciyun taṉ iṉamākak koṇṭu - māṟutaṟ poruṭṭēyām. takuti atu veṉappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruṇmaittākaliṉ avaṟṟōṭokkum. nōkkeṉpatūum avvāṟē iṉamākki nōkkutaṟporuṭṭu. ivvāṟṟāṉ iraṇ ṭeṉavumpaṭum eṭṭu meṉṟavāṟu. itaṉatu payaṉ:-ōtiya vāypāṭu eṇ ṇāṉkaṟkum iṉṉa vāypāṭum iṉṉa vāypāṭum oru poruḷaveṉṟu aṟitalum avai tattam marapiṟ poruḷtōṉṟa varum iṭaiccol eṉṟalum, oppil vaḻiyāṟ poruḷ ceyyum urupu āyiṉum iṭaic collākā, terinilaiviṉai ākum eṉpataṟivittalumeṉakkoḷka. mēlvarukiṉṟaṉavaṟṟiṟkum iḵtokkumeṉpatu.
iṉip, payavuvama vāypāṭu eṭṭaṉuḷḷum eḷḷa poruva kaḷḷa vella eṉṉum nāṉkum uvamattiṉai yiḻittaṟ poruḷavākki oṉṟā yaṭaṅkum; eṉṉai? maḻaiyaip porīic collutalum ataṉatu taṉmaikkuṇaṅ kaḷḷappaṭutalum ataṉai velkaiyum eḷḷutalum pōlvaṉa iḻiviṉaiyē kāṭṭutaliṉ. iṉi viḻaiya pulla matippa vīḻa eṉṉum nāṉkum uvamikkappaṭum poruḷiṉai uyarttāmaiyāṉum uvamattiṉai iḻittuk kūṟāmaiyāṉum avai nāṉkum oru poruḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. iv vāṟṟāṟ payavuvamam eṭṭum (289) iraṇṭāyiṉa veṉpatu.
meyyuvamam iraṇṭāṅkāl,aiyapporuṭkaṇ nāṉkun tuṇi poruṭkaṇ nāṉkumeṉa iraṇṭām. kaṭuppa maruḷa puraiya ōṭa eṉṉum nāṉkum aiyapporuḷavāki oṉṟām. ōṭaveṉpatu uvamattiṉkaṇṇum poruḷiṉkaṇṇum uṇarvu kavarntōṭiṟṟeṉṉum poruḷ tōṉṟac collaliṉ atuvum aiyameṉappaṭṭatu pōlum. iṉi ēyppa oṭṭa oṭuṅka nikarppa eṉṉum nāṉkum aiyamiṉṟi uvamamum poruḷum oṉṟeṉa uṇarvutōṉṟum vāypāṭākaliṉ ivai nāṉkum oṉṟeṉappaṭṭu ivaiyeṭṭum (290) iraṇṭāyiṉa.
iṉi, uruviṉ kaṇṇum (291) pōla oppa nēra naḷiya eṉṉum nāṉ kum maṟutalaiyiṉṟic cērntaṉaveṉṟukōṭaṟku vāypāṭāki varutaliṉ avai oṉṟeṉappaṭṭaṉa.
'naḷiyeṉ kiḷavi ceṟivu mākum' (tol. col. uri. 17)) eṉṟata ṉāṉ, ataṉoṭu cērntateṉṉum poruṭṭēyāyiṟṟu. iṉi maṟuppa kāytta viyappa nanta eṉṉum nāṉkum uvamaiyoṭu maṟutalai tōṉṟi niṟkum poruḷavākaliṉ nāṉkum oṉṟeṉappaṭṭu ivaiyeṭṭum iraṇṭāyiṉa. nantutaleṉpatu kēṭu. viyattaleṉpatu uvamaiyāṉ viyakkat takkatu poruḷeṉavē ataṉkaṇ akkuṇamiṉṟeṉa maṟuttavāṟām. kāyttaleṉpatūum uvamaiyaik kāyppittalākaliṉ atuvum maṟuttaleṉpataṉ poruḷeṉappaṭṭatu.
ivvāṟu ivaiyellān tokuppa eṭṭātalum uṇṭeṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. ivaṟṟuṭ palavaṟṟaiyuñ ceyaveṉecca vāypāṭṭāṉ ōtiyatu eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, aḵtu(665)uṭampoṭu puṇarttaleṉpataṉāṉ. iṭaiccollum uriccollum viṉaiccoṟpōla niṟkumeṉavum, ataṉāṉē peyareccamum viṉaiyeccamum muṟṟumāki niṟkumeṉavum, avaiyun terinilaiviṉaiyuvamamāy varumeṉavum aṟivittaṟkeṉka. ivaṟṟai ivvāṟu eṭṭākac collutal peritum nuṇṇuṇarviṟ ṟeṉavuṇarka.	(18)
meyppāṭu eṭṭaṉvaḻip perumaiyum ciṟumaiyum paṟṟi
uvamam tōṟṟutal
294.	perumaiyuñ ciṟumaiyu meyppāṭu eṭṭaṉ
	vaḻimaruṅku aṟiyat tōṉṟu meṉpa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya uvamam iṉṉumoruvāṟṟāṉ eṭṭeṉappaṭu meṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) perumaipaṟṟiyuñ ciṟumaipaṟṟiyum oppumai koḷḷap paṭum uvamam mēṟkūṟiya meyppāṭeṭṭaṉvaḻit tōṉṟumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
meyppāṭu eṭṭeṉpaṉa:
'nakaiyē yaḻukai yiḷivaral maruṭkai
yaccam perumitam vekuḷi yuvakai'	(tol poruḷ. 21)
eṉa meyppāṭṭiyaluḷ mēṟkūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
"kaḷavuṭam paṭunariṟ kaviḻntu nilaṅkiḷaiyā"	(akam. 16)
eṉpatu nakaiyuvamam. eṉṉai? talaimakaṉaik kaṇṭaṟiyātāḷ pōlak karantoḻukukiṉṟa parattai avaṉōṭu oppumai kaṇṭu taṉiniṉṟu viḷai yāṭum putalvaṉaik koṇṭu makiḻkiṉṟāḷai, vāyiṟkatavam maṟaintu niṉṟa talaimakaḷ 'nīyum ammakaviṟkut tāyēkāṇ' eṉṟavaḻik, kaḷavu kaṇṭa poruḷoṭu kaiyakappaṭṭa kaḷvarpōlac ceyvataṟiyātu taṭumāṟip parattai mukam vēṟupaṭṭa nilaimaiyai uvamittuc cirittamaiyiṉ nakaiyuvama māyiṟṟu.
"kalaṅkaviḻnta nāykaṉpōṟ kaḷaituṇai piṟitiṉṟip
pulampumeṉ ṉilaikaṇṭum pōkalaṉē yeṉṟiyāl"	(yā. vi. mēṟ.)
eṉpatu avalavuvamam. kalaṅkaviḻnta nīkāmaṉ pōlap pulampiṉāḷeṉṟa maiyiṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.
"peruñcelva rillattu nalkūrntār pōla
varuñcellum pērumeṉ ṉeñcu"	(muttoḷ. 88)
eṉpatu, iḷivaraluvamam; eṉṉai? talaimakaṉmāṭṭu iṉpa viḷaiyāṭ ṭeytuvār palaraiyuṅ kaṇṭu neñcu tīyap puṉkaṇeytit taṉiniṉṟu pukap peṟātu iḷivantamaiyiṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.
"īrattu ḷiṉṉavai tōṉṟi ṉiḻaṟkayattuḷ
nīruṭ kuvaḷaiven taṟṟu"	(kali. 41)
eṉpatu maruṭkai yuvamam; eṉṉai? niḻaṟkayattiṉ kuvaḷai vēvaṉa viṉmaiyiṉ iḵtu aṟputamāyiṟṟu.
"cāntakat tuṇṭeṉṟu cepput tiṟantoruvaṉ
pāmpakattuk kaṇṭa tuṭaittu"	(nālaṭi. 126)
eṉpatu accamākaliṉ accavuvamam.
"mallarai maṟañcāytta mālpōṟṟaṉ kiḷaināppaṇ
kalluyar naṉañcāraṟ kalantiyalu nāṭakēḷ"	(kali. 52)
eṉpatu perumitavuvamam.
"kūṟṟuvekuṇ ṭaṉṉa muṉpoṭu
māṟṟiru vēntar maṇṇōk kiṉaiyē"	(puṟam. 42)
eṉpatu vekuḷiyuvamam.
"pāṭic ceṉṟa paricilar pōla
vuvaviṉi vāḻi tōḻi"	(akam. 65)
eṉpatu uvakaiyuvamam piṟavumaṉṉa.
ivaṟṟāṉ eṇvakai meyppāṭum paṟṟi uvamam eṭṭeṉappaṭu meṉṟu aṟintu kūṟiṉāṉ;
"perumaiyuñ ciṟumaiyuñ ciṟappiṟ ṟīrā"	(poruḷ. 285)
eṉpuḻip peṟṟāmākaliṉ. meyppāṭeṭṭaṉvaḻit tōṉṟumeṉ ṉātu 'maruṅkaṟiya' veṉṟataṉāṟ perumaiyuñ ciṟumaiyum paṟṟi varutal vaḻimaruṅkeṉavum avaipaṟṟātu meyppāṭu eṭṭumpaṟṟi vāḷātē uvamam varutal cevviteṉavuṅ koḷḷappaṭum.
"nīruṭ kuvaḷaiven taṟṟu" (kali. 41) eṉpatu, perumai paṟṟiyatu. eṉṉai? ulakanaṭai yiṟantatōr uvamam kūṟi ataṉoṭu talaimakaṉ īrattiṉai oppittamaiyiṉ.
"kaḷavuṭam paṭunariṟ kaviḻntunilaṅ kiḷaiyā"	(akam. 16)
eṉpatu, taṉakku nikarāmal iḻitturaittamaiyiṟ ciṟumaipaṟṟi vantatām.
uvamamum meyppāṭum poruḷkaḷai aṟivippaṉavāki avai vēṟuvēṟu poruḷ aṟivittaliṉ ōttu vēṟupāṭu uṭaiyaṉavāyiṉuñ ciṟupāṉmai mayaṅkiyum varumeṉṟaṟku itu kūṟavēṇṭiyateṉpatu.(19)
uvamattāṉ poruṭku uṟṟaṉa aṟintu tuṇital
295.	uvamap poruḷiṉ uṟṟatu uṇarun
	teḷimaruṅku uḷavē tiṟattiya lāṉa.
iḵtu, eytātateytuvittal nutaliṟṟu; uvamattoṭu poruḷ ovvāteṉavum oppumai cārttik koḷḷumāṟuṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ.
(i-ḷ.) uvamapporuḷiṉ. - uvamam eṉappaṭṭa poruḷāṉ; iṉ urupu āṉ urupiṉkaṇ vantatu; uṟṟa tuṇarun teḷimaruṅkuḷa  - uvamikka varum poruṭ kuṟṟatellām aṟintu tuṇiyum pakutiyumuḷa; tiṟattiya lāṉa - aṅṅaṉam tuṇiyappaṭum poruṭṭiṟam palavākivarum ilakkaṇa vakaiyāṉ e-ṟu.
appakuti palavum uṟṟuṇarāmaṟ colliyavaḻiyum, aḵtu uṇara varumeṉpatu karuttu. 'tiṟattiyalāṉa' eṉappaṭṭa pakutiyāvaṉa: mēṟkūṟap paṭṭa meyppāṭeṭṭum paṟṟi uvamaṅkoḷḷuṅkāl, 'uṟṟatuṇarun teḷi maruṅ'keṉavē uvamāṉavaṭaikku uvamēyavaṭai kuṟaintu varuvaṉavum yātum aṭaiyiṉṟi varuvaṉavumeṉṟu ivviraṇṭum uṟṟuṇarāmaṟ colliyavaḻi, avaṟṟukkum uvamapporuḷē teḷi maruṅkāmeṉavum, vāḷātē uvamañceytu uṟṟatuṇarttātavaḻiyum atuvē teḷimaruṅkā meṉavum iṉṉōraṉṉa koḷka. utāraṇam:
"kaḷavuṭam paṭunariṟ kaviḻntu nilaṅ kiḷaiyā "	(akam. 16)
eṉṟavaḻik, kaṇṭōrkkellām perunakaiyākak kaḷavuṇṭākap paṭunariṟ kaviḻntu nilaṅkiḷaiyāveṉa uṟṟatuṇarak kūṟiyatila ṉāyiṉuṅ kaiyoṭupaṭṭa kaḷavuṭaiyārpōla niṉṟāḷeṉṉum uvamap poruḷāṉē eḷḷutaṟporuḷ tōṉṟi nakai pulappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu.
"cāṟutalaik koṇṭeṉap peṇṇīṟ ṟuṟṟeṉa
paṭṭa māri ñāṉṟa ñāyiṟṟuk
kaṭṭil niṇakku miḻiciṉaṉ kaiyatu
pōḻtūṇ ṭūciyiṉ viraintaṉṟu mātō
ūrkoḷa vanta porunaṉōṭu
ārpuṉai teriyal neṭuntakai pōrē"	(puṟam. 82)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ, uvamapporuḷākiya "pōḻtūṇṭūcikkup" pala aṭai kūṟi ataṉōṭu uvamikkappaṭum pōrttoḻiliṉai yātumōr aṭaiyiṉṟi vāḷātu kūṟiṉāṉāyiṉum, uvamapporuḷāṉē pōrt toḻiṟ kuṟṟatum uṇarakkūṟiṉāṉām; eṉṉai? uṇṭāṭṭuṅ koṭaiyum uraṉoṭu nōkki maṟuttalum mutalākiya uḷḷak karuttiṉāṉ oru kaṇattuḷḷē pala vēntarai oruṅku vēṟaṟku viraikiṉṟatu pōrttoḻileṉpatu teḷiyappaṭṭamaiyiṉ.
"uḻuta nōṉpaka ṭaḻitiṉ ṟāṅku
nallamiḻ tākanī nayantuṇṇu naṟavē"	(puṟam. 125)
eṉapatum,
"maruntukoḷ marattiṉ vāḷvaṭu mayaṅki"	(puṟam. 180)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavum avai.
iṉi, vāḷātē uvamañceytu uṟṟatuṇarttātavaḻit teḷiyu māṟu:
"uruvukaṇ ṭeḷḷāmai vēṇṭu muruḷperuntērk
kaccāṇi yaṉṉā ruṭaittu"	(kuṟaḷ. 667)
eṉpataṉuḷ, avar ceykaivaṉmai kūṟārāyiṉum accāṇiyeṉṟa uvamapporuḷ tāṉē acceykaivaṉmai kūṟiṟṟu.
"vēṉiṟ puṉalaṉṉa nuntaiyai nōvāryār"	(kali. 84)
eṉpatum atu. oḻintaṉavu maṉṉa.
iṉi. uvamattiṉ uṟṟatuṇarkaveṉṉātu 'poru' ḷeṉṟataṉāṟ poruṭku aṭutta aṭaiyum uvama aṭaikku ēṟṟatu uṇarappaṭātaṉa kaḷaiyappaṭumeṉpatu. atu,
"pācaṭai nivanta kaṇaikkā ṉeyta
liṉamī ṉiruṅkaḻi yōtam malkutoṟuṅ
kayamūḻku makaḷir kaṇṇiṉ māṉum"	(kuṟun. 9)
eṉṟavaḻip, pācaṭainivanta kaṇaikkāleṉa neytalākiya poruṭku vanta aṭaiyiraṇṭuṅ kaṇṇeṉappaṭṭa uvamattiṟkēṟpa vārāmaiyāṉ avai teḷimaruṅkilaveṉṟu kaḷaintu koḷka. oḻintaṉavu maṉṉa.
maṟṟup,
"puṟamati yaṉṉa tirumuka miṟaiñci"	
eṉṟavaḻi, akattut tōṉṟum maṟuppōlap puṟattumaṟu matikkillai yeṉpatūum puṟamatipōlak kaṟaitīrnta mukameṉpatūum uvamap poruḷiṉ uṟṟatuṇarun teḷimaruṅ keṉṟataṉāṟ kōṭumōveṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷāmaikkaṉṟē varukiṉṟa cūttirameṉpatu.	(20)
uvamapporuḷ marapupaṟṟi aṟiyappaṭum eṉal
296.	uvamap poruḷai uṇaruṅ kālai
	marīiya marapiṉ vaḻakkoṭu paṭumē.
itu, mēlataṟkoru puṟaṉaṭai; mēl uvamapporuḷāṉē uṟṟa tuṇarac ceyal vēṇṭumeṉṟāṉ; iṉi aṅṅaṉam uṇarumāṟu itu kūṟiṉāṉātaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) uvamapporuḷāṉ uṟṟatuṇaruṅkālai marīi vanta vaḻak koṭupaṭuttu aṟiyappaṭum e-ṟu.
atu varumāṟu:
"kaḷavuṭam paṭunariṟ kaviḻntunilaṅ kiḷaiyā"	(akam. 16)
eṉpatu kaḷavuṭampaṭunarkkuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu ulakattu aṭippaṭa vanta vaḻak kātalāṉ, aḵtu ētuvāka ataṉaiyum aṟintu koḷḷappaṭumeṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē, ulakattu vaḻakkiṉum aṭippaṭat tōṉṟum uvamam ākāmai yāṉ uvamapporuḷ pulappāṭu ceyyātu,
"puṟamati pōlu mukam"	
eṉṟatu pōlvatu eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu.	(21)
aṭaiyaṭutta poruṭku aṭaiyaṭutta uvamamē kūṟavēṇṭutal
297.	iraṭṭaik kiḷavi iraṭṭai vaḻittē.
iḵtu, eytātateytuvittatu, aṭaiyoṭu vanta poruḷoṭu puṇarkkumāṟu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ.
(i-ḷ.) iraṭṭaikkiḷavi - aṭaiyum aṭaiyaṭutta poruḷu meṉa iraṇṭākac collappaṭuṅ kiḷavi; iraṭṭai vaḻittē  - aṭaiyum aṭai yaṭutta poruḷumeṉa iraṇṭākki niṟuttappaṭum uvamattiṉ vaḻittu e-ṟu.
"poṉkāṇ kaṭṭaḷai kaṭuppak kaṇpiṉ
puṉkāyc cuṇṇam puṭaitta mārpiṉ" 	(perumpāṇ. 220)
eṉa varum.
taṉ paimpūṇ puṭaitta ceñcuvaṭṭiṉaiyum mārpiṉaiyum poṉ ṉuraiyōṭuṅ kallōṭum uvamittamaiyiṉ, iraṭṭaik kiḷavi iraṭṭai vaḻittāyiṟṟu. iraṭṭaik kiḷaviyeṉap poruḷiṉai muṟkūṟiyataṉāṉ, iraṇṭu poruḷiṉai oṉṟākak kūṭṭi uvamikkak karutiṉāṉ uvamattiṉaiyum iraṇṭu oṉṟākkiyē uvamikkumeṉpatu karuttu. vikārattāṉ tokku niṉṟa ummaiyāṉ, oṟṟaikkiḷaviyum iraṭṭai vaḻittāki varuvaṉa koḷka. atu,
"karuṅkāl vēṅkai vīyuku tuṟuka
lirumpulik kuruḷaiyiṟ ṟōṉṟuṅ kāṭṭiṭai"	(kuṟun. 47)
eṉa varum. irumpulikkuruḷai yoṉṟaṉaiyē tuṟukalloṭum vēṅkai vīyoṭum oppittamaiyiṉ ippeyarttāyiṟṟu.	(22)
uḷḷuṟaiyuvamam tōṉṟumāṟu
298.	piṟitoṭu paṭāatu piṟappoṭu nōkki
	muṉṉai marapiṟ kūṟuṅ kālait
	tuṇivoṭu varūun tuṇiviṉōr koḷiṉē.
itu mēlellām ēṉaiyuvamaṅ kūṟi, uḷḷuṟaiyuvamam uṇart tutal nutaliṟṟu. mēl 'icaitirinticaikku' (195) meṉappaṭṭavaṟṟiṉ pakutiyāyiṉum itaṉai āṇṭuk (196) kūṟātu īṇṭuk kūṟiṉāṉ uḷḷuṟai yuvamamākalāṉum ivvōttu uvamaviyalākalāṉumeṉpatu.
(i-ḷ.) piṟitoṭu paṭātu- uvamattoṭu uvamikkappaṭum poruḷ piṟitoṉṟu tārātu; piṟappoṭu nōkki- uvamanilaṅkaḷuṭ piṟanta piṟavikaḷoṭu cārtti nōkki; muṉṉai marapiṟ kūṟuṅkālai- karuttiṉāṉ  itaṟku iḵtu uvamameṉṟu coṉṉa marapiṉāṟ kūṟuṅ kālai; tuṇivoṭu varūum tuṇiviṉōr koḷiṉē- iṉṉa poruṭku iḵtu uvamamāyiṟ ṟeṉpatu tuṇintu koḷḷat tōṉṟum, avvāṟu tuṇintukoḷḷum uṇarvuṭaiyōr koḷḷiṉ e-ṟu.
eṉavē, aḵtu ellārkkum pulaṉaṉṟu, nalluṇarvuṭai yōrkkē pulaṉeṉpatūum, avar koḷḷac ceyya vēṇṭumeṉpa tūuṅ kūṟiyavāṟu, itaṉāṉē ceyyuḷuṭ payiṉṟu varumeṉpatu kūṟiṉāṉām. avaṟṟiṟku utāraṇam mēṟkāṭṭutum. maṟṟitaṉai uvamameṉṟa teṉṉai? uvamamum uvamikkappaṭum poruḷumāka niṟīik kūṟāṉāyiṉā ṉeṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟāṉāyiṉum uvamam pōṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ ataṉai uvamameṉṟāṉ. aḵtu oppiṉākiya peyareṉpatu; eṉṉai? ivaṟṟai uvamappōliyeṉṟum kūṟumākaliṉ.	(23)
uḷḷuṟai uvama vakai
299.	uvamap pōli ainteṉa moḻipa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya uḷḷuṟaiyuvamam aivakaippaṭumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uḷḷuṟaiyuvamam aintuvakaiyeṉak kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
avaiyaintumāmāṟu muṉṉarc(300) collum. itaṉatu payam ēṉaiyuvamattukku nilaikkaḷam aintu ōtiṉāṉ, avvāṟē itaṟkum nilaik kaḷam ōtātu (305) avai pōṟaliṉ, avaiyē nilaikkaḷamā meṉṟalum ēṉaiyuvamattuḷ oru cātiyōṭu oru cātiyiṉaiuvamittal vaḻakkaṉ ṟāyiṉum uḷḷuṟai yuvamattiṟku amaiyumeṉṟalumeṉpatu.(24)
mēṟkūṟiya uḷḷuṟai aintāvaṉa
300.	tavalaruñ ciṟappiṉat taṉmai nāṭiṉ
	viṉaiyiṉum payattiṉum uṟuppiṉum uruviṉum
	piṟappiṉum varūum tiṟattiya leṉpa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya aintum ivaiyeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉai payaṉ mey uruveṉṟa nāṉkiṉāṉum piṟappiṉā ṉum varum mēṟkūṟiya aintum e-ṟu.
uṟuppeṉṟatu meyyiṉai; uṭampiṉai uṟuppeṉpavākalāṉum meyyuvamamellām uṟuppiṉaiyēpaṟṟi varutal perumpāṉmaiyavāka lāṉum avvāṟu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. 'tavalaruñ ciṟappiṉat taṉmai nāṭiṉ' eṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉai yuvamattiṉum uḷḷuṟai yuvamamē ceyyuṭ kum poruḷilakkaṇattiṟkuñ ciṟantateṉpatu. avai varumāṟu.
"karumpunaṭu pāttik kalitta tāmarai
curumpupaci kaḷaiyum perumpuṉa lūra
putalva ṉīṉṟavem muyaṅka
latuvē teyyaniṉ mārpucitaip patuvē"	(aiṅkuṟu. 65)
eṉpatu viṉaiyumavappōli. eṉṉai? tāmaraiyiṉai viḷaippataṟ kaṉṟik karumpu naṭutaṟkuc ceyta pāttiyuḷ, tāṉē viḷainta tāmarai curum piṉ paci tīrkku mūraṉeṉṟāḷ. itaṉ karuttu, kātaṟparattaiyarkkum iṟparattaiyarkkum eṉṟamaikkappaṭṭa kōyiluḷ yāmumuḷamāki illaṟam pūṇṭu viruntōmpukiṉṟaṉam atupōla veṉpatākalāṉ uvamattiṟkup piṟitoru poruḷ etirntu uvamañ ceyyātu āṇṭup piṟantaṉavaṟṟoṭu nōkkik karuttiṉāṟ koḷḷavaittaliṉ iḵtu uḷḷuṟai yuvama māyiṟṟu. avaṟṟuḷḷum itu curumpu pacikaḷaiyun toḻiloṭu viruntōmputaṟṟoḻil uvamaṅkoḷḷa niṉṟamaiyiṉ viṉaiyuvamappōli yāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟavē itaṉai ip poruṇmait teṉpatellām uṇarumāṟeṉṉai yeṉiṉ, muṉṉart,
"tuṇivoṭu varūun tuṇiviṉōr koḷiṉē" 	(tol. poruḷ. 298)
eṉal vēṇṭiyatu itaṉ arumai nōkkiyaṉṟē yeṉpatu. allākkāṟ 'karumpunaṭu pāttik kalitta tāmarai, curumpupaci kaḷaiyum perumpuṉa lūra' eṉpatu payamila kūṟalā meṉpatu.
"karaicēr vēḻaṅ karumpiṟ pūkkun
tuṟaikē ḻūraṉ koṭumai nāṇi
nalla ṉeṉṟum yāmē
yalla ṉeṉṉumem taṭameṉ ṟōḷē1" 	
eṉpatu, payavuvamappōli. itaṉuḷ talaimakaṉ koṭumai kūṟiyatallatu akkoṭumaik kētuvākiyatoṉṟu viḷaṅkak kūṟiyatilaḷāyiṉum, iḻinta vēḻam uyarnta karumpiṟ pūkkumeṉavē, avaṟku iḻipuyarvām eṉpa toṉṟillai; ellārum iṉpaṅ kōṭaṟkuriyar talaimakaṟ keṉṟa maiyiṉ yāmum parattaiyarum avaṟku ottaṉa meṉṟamaiyiṉ avai kūṟiṉāḷeṉpatu.
"nīruṟai kōḻi nīlac cēval
kūrukirp pēṭai vayāa mūra
puḷiṅkāy vēṭkait taṉṟuniṉ
malarnta mārpivaḷ vayāa nōykkē"	(aiṅkuṟu. 51)
eṉpatum atu. nīruṟai kōḻi nīlac cēvalai ataṉ kūrukirppeṭai niṉaintatu kaṭuñcūlāṉvanta vayāt tīrtaṟ payattatākum, atupōla niṉmārpu niṉaintu taṉ vayavunōy tīrum ivaḷumeṉṟavāṟu. puḷiṅkāy vēṭkait teṉpatu, niṉ mārputāṉ ivaḷai nayavātāyiṉum ivaḷ tāṉē niṉ mārpai nayantu payampeṟṟāḷ pōlac cuvaikoṇṭu ciṟitu vēṭkai taṇitaṟ payattaḷākum, puḷiyaṅkāy niṉaiya vāynīr ūṟumāṟu pōla eṉpatu.
"oṉṟē ṉallē ṉoṉṟuveṉ kuṉṟattup
porukaḷiṟu mititta neritāḷ vēṅkai
kuṟavar makaḷir kūntaṟ peymmār
niṉṟukoya malaru nāṭaṉoṭu
oṉṟēṉ tōḻi yoṉṟi ṉāṉē"	(kuṟun. 208)
eṉpatu meyyuvamappōli; eṉṉai? mitiyuṇṭu vīḻnta vēṅkai kuṟaiyuyiroṭu malarntāṟpōla yāṉum uḷēṉāyiṉē ṉeṉṟamaiyiṉ.
" 2vaṇṇa voṇṭaḻai nuṭaṅka vāliḻai
yoṇṇuta larivai paṇṇai pāynteṉak
kaṇṇaṟuṅ kuvaḷai nāṟit
taṇṇeṉ ṟiciṉē peruntuṟaip puṉalē"	(aiṅkuṟu. 73)
iḵtu, uruvuvamappōli. nī puṉalāṭiya ñāṉṟu parattai pāyn tāṭiya puṉalellān taṇṇeṉṟateṉak kūṟiyavaḻi, attaṭampōla ivaḷ uṟakkalaṅkit teḷintu taṇṇeṉṟāḷeṉpatu karuti yuṇarappaṭṭatu. avaḷoṭu puṉal pāyntāṭiya iṉpac ciṟappuk kēṭṭu nilaiyāṟṟā ḷeṉpatu karuttu. itu niṟamaṉṟāleṉiṉ, niṟamum paṇpākaliṉ anniṟattōṭu niṟamallāta paṇpuṅ koḷḷappaṭumeṉpatu 'vakaipeṟa vanta uvamat tōṟṟam' (tol. poruḷ. 276) eṉpuḻik kūṟiṉāmeṉpatu.
"poykaip paḷḷip pulavunāṟu nīrnāy
vāḷai nāḷirai peṟūu mūra
ennalan tolaiva tāyiṉun
tuṉṉalam perumapiṟart tōynta mārpē"	(aiṅkuṟu. 63)
eṉpatu, piṟappuvamappōli. nalla kulattiṟ piṟantum iḻintārait tōyn tamaiyāṉ avar nāṟṟamē nāṟiyatu, avaraiyē pātukāvāy, mēṟkulattup piṟanta emmait tīṇṭaleṉpāḷ aḵtellām viḷaṅkak kūṟātu 'poykaip paḷḷip piṟanta nīrnāy muṉṉāḷ tiṉṟa vāḷaimīṉ pulavu nāṟṟattoṭu piṉṉāḷum ataṉaiyē vēṇṭum ūraṉ' eṉṟamaiyiṉ, parattaiyar piṟappu iḻintamaiyun talaivi piṟappu uyarntamaiyuṅ kūṟi avaṉ piṟappiṉ uyarvuṅ kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, itu piṟappuvamappōli yāyiṟṟu.
ivaiyellāṅ karutik kūṟiṟ ceyyuṭkuc ciṟappāmeṉavum, vāḷātu nīr nāy vāḷai peṟūu mūraṉeṉṟataṉāṉ oru payamiṉṟeṉavuṅ koḷka. 'piṟappoṭu varūun tiṟatta' veṉṟatu. talaimakaṉāṉ nikaḻum ivvāṟu tiṟappāṭu vēṟumuḷa veṉpatūuṅ koḷka. avai,
"taṉpārpput tiṉṉu maṉpiṉ mutalaiyoṭu
veṇpūm poykait tavaṉū reṉpa, ataṉāṉ
taṉco luṇarntōr mēṉi
poṉpōṟ ceyyu mūrkiḻa vōṉē"	(aiṅkuṟu. 41)
eṉṟavaḻit, taṉ pārpput tiṉṉum aṉpil mutalai yeṉpatu talai makaṉatu koṭumaikku uvamamāyiṟṟu. veṇpūm poykaittu avaṉū reṉpatu talaimakaḷ pacappuniṟam paṟṟi uvamamāyiṟṟu. ātalāṉ viṉai yuvamamum uruvuvamamum oru ceyyuḷuḷḷē toṭarntu oruṅku varutalum uṭaiyaveṉpatu,
iṉṉum 'tiṟattiyal' eṉṟataṉāṉē, ittaṉmaittākiya ūraṉaiyā ḷeṉac collutalun, talaivaṉūriṉkaṇuḷḷaṉa collat talaivaṟkē yaṉṟit talaivik kēṟṟa uvamam tōṉṟac ceytalum ēṉaiyuvamaṅ kūṟiyavaḻi uḷḷuṟaiyuvamaṅ kōṭalum, piṟavāṟu varuvaṉavuḷavāyiṉum ellāṅ koḷka.
"tērvaṇ kōmāṉ ṟēṉū raṉṉavivaḷ"	(aiṅkuṟu. 55)
eṉpatu avaṉūraṉaiyāḷeṉa vantatu.
"veṇpūm poykaittu avaṉūr" 	(aiṅkuṟu. 41)
eṉat talaivaṉūriṉ uḷḷatoṉṟaṉāṉ talaivik kuvamam piṟappittavā ṟāyiṟṟu. allākkāl,
"veḷḷai yāmpa laṭaikarai"	(aiṅkuṟu. 41)
eṉṟataṉāṟ payamiṉṟeṉpatu.	(25)
kiḻaviyum tōḻiyum uḷḷuṟai colla vēṇṭu muṟai
301.	kiḻavi colliṉ avaḷaṟi kiḷavi
	tōḻikku āyiṉ nilampeyarntu uraiyātu.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya uḷḷuṟaiyuvamattiṟ kāvatōr ilakkaṇam.
(i-ḷ.) aintu vakaippaṭṭa uvamappōliyum piṟitoṭu paṭātu piṟappu nōkki uṇarak kūṟiyavaḻi, akkūṟṟut talaimakaṭkun tōḻikkum urittāṅkāl, talaivikkāyiṉ avaḷaṟiyuṅ karupporuḷāṉē ceppal vēṇṭum; tōḻikkāyiṉ annilattuḷḷaṉa vellāñ collavum peṟum; piṟanilattuḷḷaṉa aṟintu colliṉaḷākac ceyyuḷ ceyyap peṟāḷ e-ṟu.
itaṉatu payam, talaimakaḷ innilattuḷḷaṉavellām aṟiyun tuṇaip payiṟciyila ḷeṉavum avaḷāyattārāyiṉ innilattuḷḷaṉa aṟiyac citainta tiṉṟeṉavum kūṟiyavāṟu.
"oṉṟē ṉallē ṉoṉṟuveṉ"	(kuṟun. 208)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuṭ, porukaḷiṟu mititta neritāḷ vēṅkai maram paṭappaiyiluḷḷa tākalāṉun taṉṉāṟ pūkkoyyappaṭu mātalāṉum aḵtu avaḷaṟikiḷavi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
"taṉpārpput tiṉṉu maṉpiṉ mutalai"	(aiṅkuṟu. 41)
eṉpatu tōḻi kūṟṟu. eṉṉai? avaṟṟiṉ ceykaiyellām aṟi yāḷaṉṟē talaimakaḷ, perumpētai yātaliṉeṉpatu. atikārattāṉiṉṟa uḷḷuṟaiyuvama meṉpataṉai avaḷaṟi kiḷavi yeṉṟataṟkup peyarp payaṉilaiyākavum nilampeyarn turaiyāteṉṉum muṟṟuviṉaikkup peyarākavum vēṟu vēṟu collikkoḷka.	(26)
talaivaṉ uḷḷuṟai kiḷakkumāṟum
oḻintōrkku iṭam varaivuiṉṟu ātalum
302.	kiḻavōṟku āyiṉ uraṉoṭu kiḷakkum
	ēṉōrkku ellām iṭamvaraivu iṉṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḻavōṉ collum uḷḷuṟaiyuvaman taṉuraṉuṭaimai tōṉṟac collappaṭum; ēṉōreṉappaṭṭa pāṅkaṉum pāṇaṉu mutalāyiṉōr colluṅkālai iṭam varaiyappaṭātu, mēṟkūṟiya vakaiyāṉē, tāntām aṟinta kiḷaviyāṉum nilampeyarnturaiyāta kiḷaviyāṉum en nilattuḷḷa poruḷāṉum uḷḷuṟaiyuvamam collap peṟupa. e-ṟu.
"karuṅkōṭ ṭerumaic ceṅkaṭ puṉiṟṟāk
kātaṟ kuḻavikku ūṟumulai maṭukkum
nuntai nummūr varutum
oṇṭoṭi maṭantai niṉṉaiyām peṟiṉē"	(aiṅkuṟu. 92)
eṉṟavaḻit, tāy pōṉṟu nummait talaiyaḷippaleṉat talaimakaṉ talaimai tōṉṟa uraṉoṭu kiḷantavāṟu kāṇka. oḻintōrkkāyiṉ varaiyaṟaiyiṉmaiyiṟ kāṭṭāmāyiṉām. avai vantuḻik (kali. 69) kāṇka.	(27)
iṉpamum tuṉpamum tōṉṟa uḷḷuṟaiyuvamam collappaṭutal
303.	iṉituṟu kiḷaviyun tuṉiyuṟu kiḷaviyum
	uvama maruṅkiṉ tōṉṟu meṉpa.
ituvum mēlataṟkē yāvatoru vēṟupāṭuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟ kūṟappaṭṭa uḷḷuṟaiyuvamam iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷ tōṉṟac collavumpaṭum e-ṟu.
"kaḻaṉi māattu viḷaintuku tīmpaḻam
paḻaṉa vāḷai katūum ūraṉ"	(kuṟun. 8)
eṉpatu, palavakai yiṉpamum varuntātu peṟuvareṉpataṟku uvamamāki varutaliṉ iṉituṟu kiḷavi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
"tāycāp piṟakkum puḷḷik kaḷvaṉoṭu
piḷḷai tiṉṉu mutalaittu"	(aiṅkuṟu. 24)
eṉpatu, talaimakaṉ koṭumai kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ tuṉiyuṟu kiḷaviyāyiṟṟu.
ivviru pakutiyum paṭac ceyyappaṭum mēṟkūṟiya uḷḷuṟai yuvamameṉpatu itaṉ karuttu.
'maruṅku' eṉṉum mikaiyāṉē, ēṉaiyuvamattiṉkaṇṇum ippakuti koḷḷappaṭum. avai,
"marunteṉiṉ maruntē vaippeṉiṉ vaippē"	(kuṟun. 71)
eṉpatu, iṉituṟu kiḷavi.
"kārattiṉ veyyaen tōḷ"	(ain. aim. 24)
eṉpatu, tuṉiyuṟu kiḷavi.	        (28)
talaivi uḷḷuṟai kūṟum nilam
304.	kiḻavōṭku uvamam īriṭattu urittē.
iḵtu, avvuḷḷuṟaiyuvamattai varaiyaṟuttuṇarttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) talaimakaḷ iraṇṭiṭattallatu uḷḷuṟaiyuvamam collap peṟāḷ e-ṟu.
iraṇṭiṭameṉpaṉa, marutamum neytalum. annilattup piṟanta poruḷ paṟṟiyallatu uḷḷuṟaiyuvamañ collutal kiḻattikkurit taṉṟeṉpatu karuttu. ivviṭattu urimaiyuṭait teṉavē kuṟiñcikkaṇ attuṇaiyurittaṉ ṟeṉṟavāṟu.
"tāmarai vaṇṭūtu paṉimala rāru mūra
yārai yōniṟ pulakkēm"	(akam. 46)
eṉṟavaḻi vaṇṭūtu paṉimalareṉap piṟarkkumuriya makaḷireṉavum avarai nayappāyeṉavum uḷḷuṟaiyuvamam marutattukkaṇ vantatu.
"aṉṉai vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉai kaḻiya
muṇṭakam malarun taṇkaṭaṟ cērppaṉ
eṉtōḷ tuṟantaṉa ṉāyiṉ
eṉṉāṅ kollava ṉayanta tōḷē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 108)
eṉpatu neytal. itaṉuṭ 'kaḻiya muṇṭakam malarum' eṉa muḷḷuṭaiya taṉaip pūmalarumeṉṟu uḷḷuṟuttataṉāṉ, iruvar kāmattuṟaik kaṇṇum orutalai iṉṉā orutalai iṉiteṉṟāḷeṉpatu. 'eṉtōḷ tuṟantaṉaṉ' eṉpatu, muḷḷuṭaimaiyōṭokka, 'eṉṉāṅkollava ṉayantōḷ tōḷ' eṉṟavaḻi avaḷ aṉpiṟṟiriyāmai kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ muṇṭaka malarcciyō ṭoppikkappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa,
"kuṉṟak kuṟavaṉ pulvēy kurampai
maṉṟā ṭiḷamaḻai maṟaikkum nāṭaṉ
puraiyōṉ vāḻi tōḻi viraipeya
larumpaṉi yaḷaiiya kūtirp
peruntaṇ vāṭaiyiṉ muntuvan taṉaṉē''	(aiṅkuṟu. 252)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟiñcippāṭṭiṉuḷ, vaṟumai kūrnta pulvēy kurampaiyai maḻai puṟamaṟaittāṟ pōla vāṭai ceyyum nōytīrkka vantā ṉeṉṟu uḷḷuṟaiyuvamañ ceytavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.
iṉik, 'kiḻavōṭ kuvamam piriviṭat turittu' eṉpatu pāṭamāka uraippārumuḷar. yātāṉumoru nilattāyiṉum pirintiruntaviṭattu uḷḷuṟaiyuvamaṅ kūṟappeṟuṅ kiḻattiyeṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. perun taṇvāṭaiyiṉ muntuvantōṉeṉpatu pirivaṉṟākaliṉ īriṭameṉṟalē valiteṉpatu.	(29)
talaivaṉ uḷḷuṟai kūṟutaṟku varaiyaṟai illai eṉal
305.	kiḻavōṟ kāyiṉ iṭamvarai viṉṟē.
mēl 'uraṉoṭu kiḷakkum' (tol poruḷ-302) eṉṟatallatu iṉṉavaḻic collappeṟun talaimakaṉeṉṟilaṉ, ataṉāṉ avaṟku ellā nilaṉum uriyavāmeṉkiṉṟāṉeṉpatu.
(i-ḷ.) talaimakaṟku iṭavaraiyaṟai illai e-ṟu.
varaiyaṟaiyillaṉavaṟṟukku varaiyaṟai kūṟāmē muṭiyāvō veṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉamāyiṉuṅ kiḻattikkun tōḻikkum iṭam (301) varaiyaṟuttataṉaik kaṇṭa māṇākkaṉ ivvāṟē talaimakaṟkum iṭam varaiyaṟai yuṇṭukoleṉṟu karutiṟ karutaṟkaveṉṟaṟku itu kūṟiṉā ṉeṉpatu.	(30)
tōḻiyum ceviliyum uḷḷuṟai kūṟumāṟu
306.	tōḻiyuñ ceviliyum poruntuvaḻi nōkkik
	kūṟutaṟ kuriyar koḷvaḻi yāṉa.
itu, tōḻiyuñ ceviliyum uḷḷuṟaiyuvamaṅ kūṟumiṭamuṇarttu tal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kālattiṟkum iṭattiṟkum poruntumāṟṟāṉ uḷḷuṟai yuvamaṅ kūṟaṟkuriyar tōḻiyuñ ceviliyum (e - ṟu).
kālamumiṭaṉum poruntutaleṉpatu veḷippaṭak kiḷavātu muṉṉattāṉ maṟaittuc collavēṇṭiyavaḻi avvāṟu colluvar avaru meṉṟavāṟu, iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟavē, 
'ēṉōrk kellā miṭamvarai viṉṟu'	(302)
eṉṟavaḻi, ellārum uḷḷuṟaiyuvamañ collaṟkuriyareṉpatu paṭṭatu. ataṉai, naṟṟāyum āyattārun tantaiyun taṉṉaiyarum uḷḷuṟai yuvamam kūṟāreṉavum, tōḻi kūṟiṉ nilam peyarnturaiyāta poruḷāṉ oruvaḻik kūṟumeṉavum, cevilikkāyiṉ iṭamvaraiviṉ ṟeṉappaṭṭa vakaiyāṉ poruntumvaḻik kūṟutaṟkuriyaḷeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉāṉām iccūt tirattāṉeṉpatu. 'koḷvaḻi'yeṉṟataṉāṉ, tōḻikkup pōla nilam peyarnturaiyāta poruḷāṉ uḷḷuṟaiyuvamaṅ kūṟutalē cevilikku muritteṉpatu koḷka. maṟṟivaiyellām akapporuṭkē yuriyavāka vitantōtiya teṉṉai puṟapporuṭku vārātaṉapōla eṉiṉ, āṇṭu varutal aritākaliṉ ivvāṟu akattiṟkē kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.
"vaṉpulak kēḷirkku varuvirun tayarum
meṉpula vaippi ṉaṉṉāṭṭup poruna"	(puṟam. 42)
eṉṟakkāṟ, pakaivēntarai veṉṟikoḷḷuṅkāl avar tāmē tattam poruḷ piṟarkkaḷippāreṉṉum poruḷ tōṉṟiṉum tōṉṟu meṉpatal latu oru talaiyāka uḷḷuṟaiyuvamaṅ kōṭal vēṇṭuvataṉṟu; eṉṉai?
"tāycāp piṟakkum puḷḷik kaḷvaṉoṭu
piḷḷai tiṉṉu mutalait tavaṉūr"	(aiṅkuṟu. 24)
eṉṟāṟpōlak kūṟātu, annāṭṭuk karuṅkaḷamar mutalāyiṉār varuntā maṟ peṟum poruḷ piṟanāṭṭārkku viruntuceyyat takumeṉṟu annāṭṭiṉatu vaḷamai kūṟiṉamaiyiṉeṉpatu. tōḻi poruntiyavaḻik kūṟumāṟu muṉ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. cevili poruntuvaḻik kūṟuvaṉavuṅ kaṇṭukoḷka. ilakkaṇamuṇmaiyiṉ avaiyum uḷaveṉpatu karuttu. 	(31)
vēṟupaṭa varum uvamaṅkaḷum koḷḷappaṭutal
307.	vēṟupaṭa vanta vuvamat tōṟṟam
	kūṟiya maruṅkiṉ koḷvaḻik koḷāal.
itu, mēlellām iruvakaiyuvamamuṅ kūṟi iṉṉum ēṉai yuvamap pakutiyē kūṟuvāṉ eytātateytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟupaṭavanta uvamattōṟṟam- vēṟupāṭu tōṉṟa vanta uvamaccāti; tōṟṟameṉiṉum piṟappeṉiṉuñ cātiyeṉiṉum okkum; kūṟiya maruṅkiṟ koḷvaḻik koḷāal- aṅṅaṉam vēṟupaṭa vantaṉavā yiṉum mēṟkūṟiya pakutiyāṉē koḷḷumiṭaṉaṟintu koḷuttuka e-ṟu.
koḷuvutaleṉpatu 'koḷāal' eṉpatāyiṟṟu.
vēṟupaṭavarutaleṉpatu; uvamattukkum poruṭkum oppumai māṟu paṭak kūṟutalum, oppumai kūṟātu peyar pōlvaṉavaṟṟu māttiraiyāṉē  maṟuttuk kūṟutalum, oppumai maṟuttup poruḷai nāṭṭik kūṟutalum, oppumai maṟuttavaḻip piṟitōruvamam nāṭṭutalum, 4uvamamum poruḷum muṟkūṟi niṟīip piṉṉar maṟṟaiya ovvāveṉṟa lum, uvamattukku irukuṇaṅkoṭuttup poruḷiṉai vāḷātu kūṟuṅkāl uvamattai iraṇṭākki oṉṟaṟkuk kūṟiya aṭai oṉṟaṟkuk kūṟātukūṟutalum, oppumai kuṟaivupaṭa uvamittu maṟṟoru kuṇaṅ koṭuttu nirapputalum, ovvāk karuttiṉāṉ oppumai kōṭalum, uvamattiṟkaṉṟi uvamattiṟku ētuvākiya poruṭkuc cila aṭaikūṟi av aṭaiyāṉē uvamikkappaṭum poruḷiṉaic ciṟappittalum, uvamāṉat tiṉai uvamēyamākkiyum atu vilakkiyuṅ kūṟutalum, iraṇṭu poru ḷāṉē vevvēṟu kūṟiyavaḻi oṉṟu oṉṟaṟku uvamameṉpatu koḷḷa vaittalum, iṉṉōraṉṉa vellām 'vēṟupaṭavanta uvamattōṟṟam' eṉappaṭum.
ivaṟṟaik 'kūṟiya maruṅkiṟ koḷuttutal' eṉpatu, muṟkūṟiya ēṉaiyuvamattiṉpālum piṟkūṟiya uḷḷuṟaiyuvamattiṉpālum paṭuttu uṇarappaṭumeṉpatu. ēṉaiyuvamattiṉpāṟpaṭuttaleṉpatu, viṉai payaṉ mey uru eṉṟa nāṉkumpaṟṟi (276) varutalum avaṟṟukku ōtiya aivakai nilaikkaḷaṉumpaṟṟi (279-280) varutalumeṉak koḷka. uḷḷuṟaiyuvamat tiṉpāṟpaṭuttaleṉpatu, ivvēṉaiyuvamampōla uvamaiyum poruḷu māki vēṟuvēṟu viḷaṅka vārātu kuṟippiṉāṟ koḷḷavarutaliṉ. ikkaruttiṉāṉē itaṉai īṇṭu vaippāṉāyiṟṟu. varalāṟu:
"vaiyaṅ kāvalar vaḻimoḻin toḻukap
pōkam vēṇṭip potuccoṟ poṟāatu
iṭañciṟi teṉṉum ūkkan turappa
voṭuṅkā vuḷḷat tōmpā vīkaik
kaṭantaṭu tāṉaic cēra lātaṉai
yāṅkaṉa mottiyō vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭilam
poḻuteṉa varaiti puṟaṅkoṭut tiṟatti
māṟi varuti malaimaṟain toḷitti
akaliru vicumpi ṉāṉum
pakalviḷaṅ kalaiyāṟ palkatir virittē''	(puṟam. 8)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ, "kaṭantaṭu tāṉaic cēralātaṉai" eṉṉun tuṇai uvamattiṟku vanta aṭaiyiṉaip poruṭku maṟuttuk koḷḷa vaittāṉeṉpatu. iṉip "poḻuteṉa varaiti" eṉpatu toṭaṅkip pāṭṭu muṭikāṟum poruṭkuriya aṭaiyiṉai uvamattiṟku maṟuttuk koḷḷa vaittāṉeṉpatu. eṉṉai? veñcuṭarvaḻit tōṉṟiya aracaṉait taṇcuṭarōṭu uvamippāṉ 'poruḷē uvamañ' ceytamaiyā ṉeṉpatu.
'poruḷē uvamañ ceytaṉar moḻiyiṉu' (284) meṉṟataṉāṟ poruḷiṉai uvamamākak kūṟātu uḷḷuṟaiyuvamam pōlak koḷḷa vaittup piṉṉar uvamattiṟku aṭaiyāyavaṟṟuḷ,
"vaiyaṅ kāvalar vaḻimoḻin toḻuka"	
eṉṟāṉ.
vaḻimoḻitaleṉpatu, vēṟṟaracarkkut tamtaṉmaiyeṉa vēṟiṉṟit taṉṉakappaṭuttal; ākalāṉ, tattam oḷiyoṭu paṭṭu oḻinta kōḷuñ cellat tāṉuñ cellum matiyameṉṟu etirmaṟuttuk kuṟṟaṅkūṟum kuṟippup paṭa vaittāṉeṉpatu.
"pōkam vēṇṭip potuccoṟ poṟāatu"	
eṉavē, iṉpanukarvumuṟṟuc ciṟappillāk kaṭṭurai yeytāṉeṉavum avaṉōṭu uvamikkiṉṟa matiyamāyiṉ irupatteḻuvar makaḷiroṭu pōkan tuyttuc ciṟappillāta kaṭṭurai puṉaiyumeṉṟum etirmaṟuttuk koḷḷa vaittāṉ. ciṟappiṉmai yeṉpatu, ellārkkum ottavāṟṟāṉ aṟañ ceyyātu urōkaṇimēṟ kaḻiperuṅ kātalaṉeṉappaṭutal pōlvaṉa.
"iṭañciṟi teṉṉu mūkkan turappa"	
eṉavē, eññāṉṟun taṉṉellaikkaṇṇē varum mati maṇṭila meṉṟu etirmaṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭum. "oṭuṅkāvuḷḷam" eṉavē, ammatiyam tēyn toṭuṅkumeṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum. "ōmpā vīkai" eṉavē, nāṭōṟum orōvoru kalaiyākap palluyirkkum iṉpam payakkumāṟṟāṉ taruva tallātu tāṉuṭaiyatellām orukālē koṭāta matiyameṉappaṭum."kaṭan taṭu tāṉai" eṉavē, matikkut tāṉaiyākiya tārakaiyellām pakaikkatirākiya paruti maṇṭilattukkut tōṟku meṉṟāṉām. ivvāṟṟāṉ uvamāṉa aṭaiyellām etir maṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉip, poruṭkuriya aṭaiyum avvāṟē etirmaṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭumeṉṟavāṟu. "vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭila" meṉavē, kaṭaiyā yiṉār katiyiṟ cellu matiyameṉṟu pāṭṭuṭait talaivaṉ talaiyāyi ṉār katiyiṟ cellu meṉṟāṉ. "poḻuteṉa varaiti" yeṉavē, nāṭōṟum nāḻikai vēṟupaṭṭu eṟitti eṉṟataṉāṉ avaṉ poḻutu ceyyāṉeṉavum, "puṟak koṭut tiṟatti" yeṉavē, tōṟṟōr pōṉṟu oḷi maḻuṅkic celkiṉṟāy eṉpataṉāṉ cuṭar pōla viḷaṅkip piṟar tōṟṟōṭak kāyum ivaṉeṉavum, "māṟi varuti" yeṉavē, tiṅkaṭōṟum māṟip piṟatti yeṉpataṉāṉ ivaṉ nilaipeṟṟā ṉeṉavum, "malai maṟain toḷitti" yeṉavē, malaicārntavaḻit tōṉṟāyeṉpa taṉāṉ ivaṉ taṉṉāṭṭu malai mīkkūṟumeṉavum. "akaliru vicumpiṉāṉu" meṉavē, ivaṉ ivvulakattum nilaipēṟuṭaiyaṉeṉavum, "pakal viḷaṅkāy" eṉavē, ivaṉ iru poḻutum viḷaṅkumeṉavuṅ koḷḷap paṭum. muṟpakutiyum piṟpakutiyum vēṟupaṭutaliṉ vēṟupaṭa vantatāyiṟṟu. maṟṟaiyavum aṉṉa.
ikkūṟiya aṭai yellām, viṉai payaṉ meyuruveṉak 'kūṟiya maruṅkiṟ koḷvaḻik koḷumuvavamākak' kāṭṭuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum iḵtok kum.
"kaṇṇa ṉavaṉivaṉ māṟaṉ kamaḻtuḻāyk
kaṇṇi yavaṟkivaṟku vēmputār - vaṇṇamum
māya ṉavaṉivaṉ cēyaṉ marapoṉṟē
āya ṉavaṉivaṉ kō"	
eṉpatu, peyarun tāru mutalāyiṉa paṟṟi māyaṉōṭu uvamaṅkaruti maṟut turaittavāṟu. peyareṉpatu poruḷuṇarttutaliṉ ataṉai vaṭiviṉ pāṟ paṭuttuṇarka.
"aṭinōkki ṉāḻkaṭal vaṇṇaṉṟaṉ mēṉip
paṭinōkkiṟ paiṅkoṉṟait tārōṉ- muṭinōkkit
tērvaḷava ṉāta ṟeḷintēṉṟaṉ ceṉṉimēl
āralaṅka ṟōṉṟiṟṟuk kaṇṭu"	
eṉpataṉuḷ, āḻkaṭal vaṇṇaṉaiyuṅ koṉṟaittārōṉaiyum uvamam kūṟiyavaṟṟai maṟuttut tērvaḷavaṉeṉat teḷintēṉeṉap poruḷaiyē nāṭṭutaliṉ aḵtu, uvamam vēṟupaṭa vantatāyiṟṟu.
"intira ṉeṉṉi ṉiraṇṭēkaṇ ṇēṟūrnta
vantarattā ṉeṉṉiṟ piṟaiyillai - yantarattuk
kōḻiyā ṉeṉṉiṉ mukamoṉṟē kōtaiyai
āḻiyā ṉeṉṟuṇaraṟ pāṟṟu"	
eṉpataṉuḷ, intiraṉaiyum iṟaiyōṉaiyum murukaṉaiyum oppu maṟuttu, neṭiyōṉai uvamaṅkūṟaliṉ, oppumai maṟuttup piṟitu nāṭṭiyatu.
"cuṟṟuviṟ kāmaṉuñ cōḻar perumakaṉāṅ
koṟṟappōrk kiḷḷiyuṅ kēḻovvār - poṟṟoṭīi
yāḻi yuṭaiyāṉ makaṉmāyaṉ; cēyaṉē
kōḻi yuṭaiyāṉ makaṉ."	
eṉpataṉāṉ, uvamamum poruḷum muṉ oruṅkuniṟīip piṉṉar ovvāmai kūṟutaliṉ ituvum piṉṉum vēṟupaṭa vantatāyiṟṟu.
"puṉaṉāṭar kōmāṉum pūntuḻāy mālum
viṉaivakaiyāṉ vēṟu paṭupa - puṉaṉāṭa
ṉēṟṟeṟintu māṟṟalarpā leytiyapār māyavaṉ
ēṟṟirantu koṇṭamaiyā ṉiṉṟu"	
eṉpatum atu.
"ūrkkuṟu mākkaḷ veṇkōṭu kaḻāaliṉ
nīrttuṟai paṭiyum peruṅkaḷiṟu pōla
iṉiyai peruma emakkē"	(puṟam. 94)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ uvamamākiya poruḷiṉai yāṉaiyuṅ kaṭāmumeṉa iraṇṭākki yāṉaikkē ūrkkuṟumākkaḷ veṇkōṭukaḻāliṉ nīrt tuṟaipaṭiyumeṉṉum aṭaikūṟi ūrkkuṟumākkaḷ pōlvārait tuṉṉaruṅ kaṭāattiṟkuc collāmaiyiṉ ituvum vēṟupaṭa vanta uvamattōṟṟa meṉappaṭṭatu.
"mutirkōṅkiṉ mukaiyeṉa mukañceyta kurumpaiyeṉap
peyaṟuḷi mukiḻeṉap peruttaniṉ ṉiḷamulai"	(kali. 56)
eṉṟavaḻi, mutirkōṅkiṉ mukaiyum muṟṟiya kurumpaiyum periyavāka liṉ avaipōlap perutta niṉ iḷamulaiyeṉṟal ottatu, peyaṟuḷi mukuḷañ ciṟitāka avaṟṟōṭu ataṉai uṭaṉkūṟi ippeyaṟuḷi mukuḷat tiṟkillāta perumaikkuṇam poruṭkup piṉṉar vitantu kūṟutaliṉ atuvum vēṟupaṭavanta uvamaiyāyiṟṟu.
"makkaḷē pōlvar kayava ravaraṉṉa
voppāri yāṅkaṇṭa til''	(kuṟaḷ. 1071)
eṉpatu ovvāpporuḷai oppumaikoṇṭatu. eṉṉai? makkaḷaik kayavar ovvāreṉṉuṅ karuttiṉāṉ makkaḷ pōlvar kayavareṉṟamai yiṉ atuvum vēṟupaṭavanta uvamamāyiṟṟu.
"neṭuntōṭ ṭirumpaṉai nīrniḻal puraiyak 
kuṟumpala murinta kuṉṟucēr ciṟuneṟi"	
eṉpataṉuḷ, uvamamākiya niḻal poruṭkeytiyatu viṉai uvamamaṉṟē? ataṟku neṭuntōṭum perumaiyum aṭaiyākak kūṟiṉāṉ; kūṟavē, paṉainiḻalōṭokkuṅ kuṉṟañcēr ciṟuneṟi muṭintavaḻic ceṉṟu pukum ūrkkuvamam neṭuntōṭeṉṟu koḷḷa vaittamaiyiṉ, atuvum vēṟupaṭavanta uvamamāyiṟṟu.
"maṇpaṭutōṭ kiḷḷi matavēḻam māṟṟaracar
veṇkuṭaiyait tēytta vekuḷiyāl - viṇpaṭarntu
pāyuṅko leṉṟu paṉimatiyun taṉṉuruvan
tēyun teḷivicumpi ṉiṉṟu"	
eṉpataṉuḷ, uvamāṉattiṉai uvamēyamākki ataṉaiyē vilakkiṉāṉ. eṉṉai? veṇkuṭaiyeṉṟu yāṉai kuttumeṉṟu matiyiṉaik kuṭai yuṭaṉoppippāṉ, matiyiṉaik kuṭaiyākavē kūṟit, 'tēyunteḷivicum piṉiṉṟu' eṉpataṉāṟ kuṭaiyōṭu uvamam kūṟiyatai vilakkiṉamaiyiṉ atuvum vēṟupaṭavanta uvamattōṟṟamāyiṟṟu.
"akara mutala veḻuttellām āti
pakavaṉ mutaṟṟē yulaku"	
eṉṟavaḻi iraṇṭu poruḷ vēṟuvēṟu kūṟiyataṉṟi, akara mutala eḻuttellām atupōla eṉṟāṉum, ātipakavaṉ mutaṟṟu ulakam atupōla eṉ ṟāṉum oṉṟākat tuṇiyumāṟṟāṉ uvamamum poruḷuṅ kūṟāmaiyiṉ, atuvum vēṟupaṭa vanta uvamamāyiṟṟu. piṟavum aṉṉa. ivai ēṉai yuvamattiṟkellām potuvilakkaṇam.	(32)
ovvāteṉak kūṟi uvamamākkutal
308.	orīik kūṟalu marīiya paṇpē.
ituvum, ēṉaiyuvamattiṟkāvatōr ilakkaṇamuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) orīikkūṟalum- okkumeṉak kūṟātu ovvāteṉak kūṟuta lum; ummai iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu; marīiya paṇpu-atuvum uvamamātaṟku aṭippaṭavanta vaḻakku e-ṟu.
utāraṇam:
"yāṅkaṉa mottiyō vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭilam"	(puṟam. 8) 
eṉavum,
"mātar mukampō loḷiviṭa vallaiyēṟ
kātalai vāḻi mati"	(kuṟaḷ. 1118)
eṉavum varum.
"niṉṉō raṉaiyainī"	(paripāṭal 1)
eṉpatum atu.
ivaṟṟuḷ, yāṅṅaṉam ottiyōveṉpatu ovvāyeṉṉum poruṭṭu. 'kātalai vāḻimati' yeṉṟavaḻiyum yāṉ kātaliyāmaiyāṉ matiyamē avaḷ vāṇmuka movvāyeṉṟaliṉ orīik kūṟiṟṟu. ituvum marīiya paṇpākalāṉum uḷḷuṟaiyuvamampōlak kuṟippiṉāṉ uvamaṅ kōṭa loppumaiyāṉum īṇṭu vaittāṉeṉpatu. paṇpeṉṟataṉāṉ atuvum ilakkaṇattōṭokkum eṉṟavāṟu. ivaṟṟaiyum vēṟupaṭa vanta uvamameṉṉāmōveṉiṉ, uvamamum avvaḻi māṟupaṭa varumāṟu vēṟupaṭavanta uvamattuk kūṟiṉāṉ; uvamamiṉmai kūṟutalum uvamameṉappaṭu meṉṟaṟku itu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. ivaṟṟuḷḷum vēṟupaṭa vanta ilakkaṇam mēlaiccūttirattuḷ aṭaṅkum.	(33)
poruṭṭup perumaiyum ciṟumaiyum tōṉṟāmalum
uvamam kūṟappaṭutal
309.	uvamat taṉmaiyum uritteṉa moḻipa
	payaṉilai purinta vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḵtu, eytātatu eytuvittatu; mēl ēṉaiyuvamaṅ kūṟuṅkāl uvamikkappaṭum poruṭkup perumaiyuñ ciṟumaiyuñ ciṟappak kūṟal vēṇṭumeṉṟāṉ, avvāṟaṉṟiyum uvamam koḷḷappaṭumeṉ ṟamaiyiṉ.
(i-ḷ.) uvamattaṉmaiyum uritteṉa moḻipa- vikāravakaiyāṟ perumaiyuñ ciṟumaiyum oruporuṭkuk kūṟātu paṭṭāṅku uvamaṅ kūṟutalum uritteṉṟu colluvar āciriyar; payaṉilai purinta vaḻakkattāṉa- ataṉāṉum oru payaṉ tōṉṟac colluta ṉeṟippāṭ ṭiṉkaṇ e-ṟu.
atu,
"pāri pāri yeṉṟupala vētti
yoruvaṟ pukaḻvar cennāp pulavar"	(puṟam. 107)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ ulakaḷittaṟku māriyum uṇṭeṉa, māriyai uvamittuc ciṟappittukkūṟuvāṉ, māriyaic ciṟappittup pāṭṭuṭait talaivaṉākiya pāriyai uyarttuk kūṟātāṉ pōla iyalpiṉāṉ uvamam kūṟiṉāṉām; itu mārikkum pārikkum ōriḻivillaiyeṉṉun taṉmaip paṭak kūṟavē, avaṉuyarvu kūṟutaliṟ payaṉilaipurinta vaḻakkeṉap paṭumeṉpatu.
"kolviṉaip polinta kūrṅkuṟum puḻukiṉ
villōr tūṇi vīṅkap peyta
appunuṉai ēyppa arumpiya iruppaic
ceppaṭa raṉṉa ceṅkuḻai yakantō
ṟiḻuti ṉaṉṉa tīmpuḻal tuyvāy
uḻutukāṇ tuḷaiya vāki ārkaḻalpu
āli vāṉiṟ kāloṭu pāṟit
tuppi ṉaṉṉa ceṅkōṭ ṭiyaviṉ
neyttōr mīmicai niṇattiṟ parikkum . . . " 	(akam. 9)
eṉpatum,
"nīḷarai yilavat talaṅkuciṉai payanta
pūḷaiyam pacuṅkāy puṭaivirin taṉṉa
varippuṟa vaṇiloṭu karuppai yāṭātu
yāṟṟaṟal puraiyum verinuṭaik koḻumaṭal
vēṟṟalai yaṉṉa vainnuti neṭuntōṭṭu
īttilai vēynta veyppuṟak kurampai"	(perumpāṇ. 83-8)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavum atu.
"kolviṉaip polinta"	(akam. 9)
eṉpataṉuḷ, innilattiṉ makkaḷ ampiṉai uvamamākki āṇṭaiya vākiya iruppaiyiṉ pūṅkottai uvamikkappaṭum poruḷākkiyum, uvama nilattiṟkēṟṟa veṇṇeyttiraḷoṭu kaḻaṉṟa pūviṉai uvamañ ceytum, annilattiyalpu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ, atu payaṉilaipurinta vaḻak keṉap paṭṭatu. piṟavum aṉṉa.
'uvamattaṉmaiyum' eṉṟa ummaiyāṉ uvamattaṉmaiyēyaṉṟi vāḷātu taṉmai kūṟutalum annilattiṟkē payaṉilaiyeṉappaṭuvaṉavuṅ koḷka; avai,
"māṉṟōṟ paḷḷi makavoṭu muṭaṅki
yīṉpiṇa voḻiyap pōki nōṉkā
ḻirumputalai yātta tiruntukaṇai viḻukkō
luḷivāyc curaiyiṉ miḷira miṇṭi 
yirunilak karampaip paṭunī ṟāṭi
nuṇpul laṭakkiya veṇpa leyiṟṟiyar
pārvai yātta paṟaitāḷ viḷavi
ṉīḻaṉ muṉṟi ṉilavuraṟ peytu
kuṟuṅkā ḻulakkai yōcci"	(perumpāṇ. 89-97)
eṉṟavaḻi uvamañceyyātu annilattiyalpu kūṟappaṭṭatāyiṉum, uvamat tāṟ poruṭpeṟṟi tōṉṟac ceytāṟpōla annilattiṟkup payappāṭu veḷippaṭac ceyyāmaiyiṉ, uvamavilakkaṇattuḷ itaṉai ilēciṉāṟ koṇṭāmeṉpatu; eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ,
"marapē tāṉum"	(tol. cey. 80)
eṉpuḻi nāṟcolliyaleṉac coṟṟaṉmaiyum (poruḷ taṉmaiyum) kūṟappaṭṭamaiyiṉ uvamaārāycciyuḷ atu (poruḷ taṉmai) kūṟāṉeṉpatu. ummai iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu; eṉṉai? uyarntataṉ mēṟṟaṉṟi uyarpiḻipuṭaittallāta taṉmaiyuvamamuṅ koḷka veṉṟamaiyiṉ.	(34)
taṭumāṟu uvamamum nīkkappaṭāmai
310.	taṭumā ṟuvamam kaṭivarai yiṉṟē.
ituvum. uvamattiṟkēyāvatōr ilakkaṇamuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) taṭumāṟuvamam- uvamamum poruḷum vēṟu niṟīi itu pōlum ituveṉṉātu avviraṇṭaṉaiyum uvamamuṟac collun taṭu māṟuvamam;  iṉi avvāṟaṉṟi uvamattaip poruḷākkiyum poruḷai uvama mākkiyun taṭumāṟac collutalun taṭumāṟuvamameṉappaṭum; kaṭivarai iṉṟu- avviraṇṭum uvamameṉṟu collaṟpāṭṭiṟ kaṭiyappaṭā e-ṟu.
"arimalar āyntaka ṇammā kaṭaici
tirumukamun tiṅkaḷuñ cettut - terumantu
vaiyattum vāṉattuñ cellā taṇaṅkāki
yaiyattu niṉṟa tarā"	(poykaiyār)
eṉpataṉuḷ, uvamaiyiṉaiyum poruḷiṉaiyum vēṟu vēṟu tuṇiyātu aiyuṟṟu vaiyattum vāṉattuñ cellātu arāveṉṟamaiyiṉ itu taṭumāṟuvamamāyiṟṟu.
"taḷipeṟṟu vaikiya taṇcuṉai nīlam
aḷipeṟṟār kaṇpōṉ malaru- maḷipeṟṟa
nallār tirumukat tōṟṟat taḷipeṟṟa
kallāram pōṉmalaruṅ kaṇ"	
eṉpuḻi, nīlattoṭu kaṇṇiṉaiyum kaṇṇiṉoṭu nīlattiṉaiyum oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu uvamamākkiyum poruḷākkiyum oruṅkē taṭumāṟak kūṟiṉamai yiṉ ituvun taṭumāṟuvamameṉappaṭṭatu. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(35)
uvamaikku uvamai illai yātal
311.	aṭukkiya tōṟṟam viṭuttal paṇpē.
iḵtu, eytiyatu maṟuttatu; eṉṉai? vēṟu oru poruḷōṭu oru poruḷai uvamittu niṟīi apporuḷoṭu piṟitoru poruḷai yuvamittalum uvamameṉṟu koḷḷuvāṉāyiṉum atu koḷḷappaṭātu, poruḷ viḷaṅkāmaiyiṉeṉak kūṟi vilakkiyamaiyiṉ.
(i-ḷ.) aṭukkiya tōṟṟam - uvamamum poruḷum niṟutti aṭukkiya tōṟṟam; viṭuttal paṇpu - ciṟappiṟṟākak koḷḷappaṭātu 
e-ṟu.
"matiyat taṉṉa vāṇmukam pōlum
potiyaviḻ tāmaraip putuppūm poykai"	
eṉṟakkāl, matiyattaṉṉa vāṇmukattiṉait tāmarai yeṉṟamaiyiṉ avai oṉṟu oṉṟaṉoṭu poruntāveṉpatu karuttu.
"ilaṅkuvaḷai yaṉṉa nalaṅkē ḻāmpaṟ
pōti ṉaṉṉa tātaviḻ kaitai"	
eṉṟakkāl, oṉṟu oṉṟaṉōṭu oruvaṇṇattatāy uvamattiṟkēṟpiṉum oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu uvamamāy niṉṟatu. niṉṟuṟa maṟṟu ataṉōṭu uvamaṅ koḷḷappaṭātu. itu varaiyaṟaiyuṭaimaiyiṉ vilakkappaṭṭatu. maṟṟu,
"īrntu nilantōyu mirumpiṭit taṭakkaiyiṟ
cērntuṭaṉ ceṟinta kuṟaṅkiṟ kuṟaṅkeṉa
mālvarai yoḻukiya vāḻai vāḻaip
pūveṉap polinta vōti yōti
naḷicciṉai vēṅkai nāṇmalar nacci"	(ciṟupāṇ. 19 - 23)
eṉpatūum aṭukkiyatōṟṟa meṉappaṭātōveṉiṉ, paṭātaṉṟē; yāṉaik kaipōluṅ kuṟaṅku; kuṟaṅkupōlum vāḻaiyeṉa aṭukkic collātu kuṟaṅkiṉaiyuṭaiyāḷeṉṟu tuṇittuk kūṟiya piṉṉark kuṟaṅkeṉa mālvarai oḻukiya vāḻai yeṉṟāṉātaliṉeṉpatu. 'tōṟṟa' meṉṟataṉāṉ uvamamum poruḷumāka niṟīi uvamavurupu tōṉṟak kūṟuṅkāl aṭukkappaṭuvateṉpatu.	(36)
uvamam niralniṟaiyākavum varumāṟu
312.	niraṉiṟuttu amaittal niraṉiṟai cuṇṇam
	varainilai vaitta mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē.
ituvum eytātaṉa varaiyaṟaiyuṭaimaiyiṟ kūṟiyavaṟṟōṭu iṉamākaliṉ uvamattiṟkāvatoṉ ṟuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) niraṉiṟuttu amaittal niraṉiṟai- uvamattaiyum niralē niṟuttup poruḷiṉaiyum niralē niṟuttu oppumai kūṟiṉ atu niraṉiṟai yeṉappaṭum; cuṇṇam varainilai vaitta mūṉṟalaṅkaṭaiyē- cuṇṇat tiṉai varainta nilaimaiyāṉ vanta cuṇṇamum aṭimaṟiyum moḻi māṟṟu meṉṉum mūṉṟumallāta iṭattu e-ṟu.
cuṇṇattiṉai varainta nilaimaiyāṉ vantaṉaveṉavē cuṇṇan tavira mūṉṟuḷavōveṉiṉ, ataṉai (mūvaruḷ) oruvarai vilakka mūvarum vantila reṉṟāṟpōlak koḷka. utāraṇam :
"koṭikuvaḷai koṭṭai nucuppuṇkaṇ mēṉi
matipavaḷa mutta mukamvāy muṟuval
piṭipiṇai maññai naṭainōkkuc cāyal 
vaṭiviṉaḷē vañci makaḷ"	
eṉa varum.
niraṉiṟuttaleṉṉātu 'amaitta' leṉṟataṉāṉ niraṉiṟaiyaṉṟi amaittuk koḷvatum uṇṭu; atu,
"kaḷiṟuṅ kantum pōla naḷikaṭaṟ
kūmpuṅ kalaṉun tōṉṟun
tōṉṟaṉ maṟantōr tuṟaikeḻu nāṭṭē"	
eṉa varum. piṟavum aṉṉa.
eṉṟārkku, 'niraṉiṟut tamaittal niraṉiṟai ēṉai, varainilai vaitta mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē' eṉṉātu cuṇṇattiṉai varaintōtiyatu eṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, avai mūṉṟuñ cuṇṇam pōlac cuṇṇañ ceyyap paṭutaliṉ avvāṟṟāṟ citarntu kiṭappa uvamam kūṟiṉ atu poruḷ viḷakkāteṉpatu aṟivittaṟkeṉpatu. eṉṉai? aṭimaṟiyuḷ ōraṭiyuḷ uvamaṅ kūṟi ōraṭiyuṭ poruḷvaittāl iṉitu poruḷkoḷḷātu aṭimaṟikkuṅ kālaip piṟitupiṟitāka luṭaittu. iṉi ōraṭiyuḷ uvamaṅ kūṟip piṉṉar ettuṇaiyuñ ceṉṟu poruḷkūṟi moḻimāṟṟik koḷḷa vaippiṉ atuvum uvamattāṟ poruḷ tōṉṟātu. cuṇṇat tiṟkum aḵtokkum.
iṉi, ivvōttiṉiṟ kūṟukiṉṟa uvamaṅkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟaiyuñ collatikārattiṉuḷḷuñ ceyyuḷiyaluḷḷuñ collukiṉṟa cila poruḷkaḷaiyum vāṅkikkoṇṭu maṟṟavai ceyyuṭkaṇṇē aṇiyā meṉa, ikkālattāciriyar nūl ceytārumuḷar. avai orutalaiyākac ceyyuṭku aṇiyeṉṟu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟappaṭā. eṉṉai? vallār ceyyiṉ aṇiyākiyum allār ceyyiṉ aṇiyaṉṟākiyum varum, tāṅkāṭṭiya ilakkaṇattiṟ citaiyāvaḻiyu meṉpatu. eṉṉai?
"2nāyakarkku nāykaḷpō ṉaṭpiṟ piṟaḻātu
kūuyk kuḻāa muṭaṉkoṭku - māypaṭaip
paṉṟi yaṉaiyar pakaivēnta rāṅkavar
ceṉṟevaṉ ceyvar ceru"	
eṉṟavaḻi, nāypōlum naṭpuṭaiyar paṭaiyāḷareṉpatu viṉaiyuvamam; paṉṟiyaṉaiyar pakaivēntareṉpatu nāykkup pakaiyākiya paṉṟipōla veṉpatu.vēṟṟuvēntar pakaivarātalāṉ avvuvamai vilakkaritu. aṉṟā māyiṉum aḵtaṇiyeṉappaṭātu, uvamamtāṉ uyarnta taṉmai yiṉ. atu kuṟṟamaṉṟōveṉiṉ,
"pērū raṭṭa kaḷḷiṟku
ōril kōyiṟ ṟērumā ṉiṉṉē"	(puṟam. 300)
eṉpatu kuṟṟamaṉṟākaliṉ atuvuṅ kuṟṟamaṉṟeṉappaṭum. iṉip poṉ mālai pōlap pūmālaiyum polivu ceytaliṉ itu kuṟṟamākātu; mēlatē kuṟṟameṉpatu. aṟṟaṉṟu, iṉṉacollum iṉṉaporuḷu muṭaiyaṉa poṉmālaiyeṉavum pūmālaiyeṉavum varaiyaṟuttuk kūṟaliṉmaiyiṉ ataṉāṉum ataṉaik kuṟṟameṉṟu ilakkaṇattār kūṟiṉ nirampātu. allatūum poruḷatikārattuṭ poruṭpakutikaḷel lāñ ceyyuṭku aṇiyākalāṉ, avai pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟavai yeṉap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ avaiyellān tokuttu aṇiyeṉak kūṟātu vēṟu cilavaṟṟai varaintu aṇiyeṉak kūṟutal payamil kūṟṟā meṉpatu.
iṉi, iraṇṭu poruḷeṇṇi, avaṟṟai viṉaippaṭukkuṅkāl oruṅkeṉpatoru coṟpeytal ceyyuṭku aṇiyeṉpa. piṟavum iṉṉōraṉṉa palavuñ ceyyuṭ kaṇiyāmeṉpatu avar karuttu. oruṅku yeṉpatēyaṉṟi mūṉṟu tāḻicaiyuḷ mūṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi 'eṉavāṅku' eṉpatoru collāṉ muṭintavaḻi 'eṉavāṅku' eṉpatoru moḻi, 'eṉaveṉpatu ōralaṅkāra' meṉalum vēṇṭumākalāṉ avvāṟu varai yaṟuttuk kūṟalamaiyāteṉpatu. piṟavum aṉṉa.
iṉi, avaṟṟaip poruḷuṟuppeṉpatallatu aṇiyeṉpavāyiṟ, cāttaṉaiyuñ cāttaṉāṉaṇiyappaṭṭa muṭiyuntoṭiyum mutalāyavaṟṟai yum vēṟu kaṇṭāṟpōla avvaṇiyuñ ceyyuḷiṉ vēṟākal vēṇṭu meṉpatu.
iṉic, ceyyuṭkaṇi ceyyum poruṭpaṭai ellāṅ kūṟātu cilavē kūṟi oḻiyiṉ atu kuṉṟakkūṟalā (663) meṉpatu. avai yāvai yeṉiṉ,
"akaṉamar kēḷva ṉakaṟci tīrttaṟku
makaṉoṭu pukunta makavunilai yeṉāa
maṟukkuṅ kālai maṟutturai moḻiyātu
kuṟippuvēṟu koḷīiya kuṟippunilai yeṉāap
pulavik kaṇṇum pōkkiṉ kaṇṇu
maḻutalu maḻāa tuyaṅkalu meṉṟāṅ
kiruvakaip paṭṭa maṅkala meṉāap
pulampuṟu kālai yaṟivoṭu paṭātu
pulampukoḷa vanta ceyviṉai yeṉāa
viṉṉō raṉṉa palporuṭ pakuti
naṉṉeṟip pulavar nāṭṭalvakai yuṭaiya"	
eṉṟoru cūttirañ ceyyiṉ avaiyum alaṅkārameṉap paṭumeṉpatu. avaṟṟukku utāraṇam :
"ākat toṭukkiya putalvaṉ puṉṟalait
tūnīr payanta tuṇaiyamai piṇaiyal"	(akam. 5)
eṉpatu, makavunilai.
"ōvac ceytiyi ṉeṉṟuniṉain toṟṟi"	(akam. 5)
eṉpatu, kuṟippunilai; eṉṉai? talaimakaṉ pōkkiṉai uvakkuṅ kuṟip pallāta kuṟippākaliṉ aḵtaṇiyeṉappaṭum.
"tummuc ceṟuppa vaḻutāḷ"	(kuṟaḷ. 1318)
eṉpatu. pulaviyuḷaḻuta maṅkalam.
"pāvai māytta paṉinīr nōkkamoṭu
. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
mōyiṉa ḷuyirtta kālai''	(akam. 5)
eṉpatu, pōkkiṉkaṇ aḻāta maṅkalanilai.
"viḷinilai kēḷā ṭamiyaṇ meṉmela
nalamiku cēvaṭi nilamvaṭuk koḷāak
kuṟuka vantutaṉ kūreyiṟu tōṉṟa"	(akam. 5)
eṉpatu, aṟivoṭu paṭātu pulampukoḷa vanta ceyviṉai; eṉṉai? kēḷātu kēṭṭāḷpōl vantamaiyiṉ. atuvum alaṅkārameṉappaṭu meṉṟu cūttirañ ceytukoḷḷal vēṇṭum, ceyyuṭku aṇi vēṟu kūṟiṉeṉpatu.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉavellāṅ kuṟṟameṉṟu koḷḷappaṭā; eṉṉai? vēṟu kāraṇamuṇarappeṟātu piṟaḻntu iṭaiyaṟavupaṭṭa kālai iṭarppaṭṭuc ceytaṉavātalāṉ, avaiyum avvāṟṟāṉamaiyu meṉpatu.
iṉi āṉantavuvamai yeṉpaṉa cila kuṟṟam akattiyaṉār ceytā reṉak kūṟupavākaliṉ avaiyiṟṟai evvāṟu kōṭumeṉiṉ, avaitām akattuḷḷum piṟacāṉṟōr ceyyuḷuḷḷum varutaliṟ kuṟṟamākā. akattiyaṉārāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa mūṉṟu tamiḻiṉum aṭaṅkāmai vēṟu āṉantavōtteṉpatu oṉṟu ceytārāyiṉ, akattiyamun tol kāppiyamum nūlāka vanta cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷ kuṟṟam vēṟupaṭāveṉpatu. aḵtēṟ, ceyyuṭku ivai yaṇiyeṉpatūum ivai kuṟṟameṉpatūum mūṉṟatikārattuḷḷum ivvāciriyar yāṇṭuṅ kūṟārōveṉiṉ, cey yuḷiyalil muppattunāṉ kuṟuppuk kūṟi, avaṟṟatiyaipu nallicaip pulavar ceyyuḷuṟuppeṉavum avai toṭarnta toṭarnilai eṇvakaiyāṉ toṭarnilaic ceyyuṭku vaṉappeṉavum pakuttu, ōtiya ilakkaṇattiṟ piṟaḻntavai ellāṅ kuṟṟameṉpatūum koḷḷavaittāṉeṉpatu.	(37)
uvamaviyaṟkup pērāciriyarurai muṟṟiṟṟu.
